# PREFACE AND ACKNOW JUDGEMENTS
## Aum Uchishta mahaganapatheye namah

The Author has done B.A (History, Politics and Sociology) and also done M.A. (Astrology), and was instrumental in establishing a Publishing firm by name "Nairs Publishing House" in Hyderabad.

In this span of almost 16 years, Author was blessed by Almighty God to have the time and knowledge to write more than 25 books on this subject in English, and translate them into in various languages such as Hindi, Telugu and Malayalam.

Author was also able to run a magazine on Astrology titled 'ASTROVISION" for the 07 years, besides authoring more than 100 articles. As of April 2020, this magazine is re-commenced its publication. The author has been conducting classes also either through mails, video chat, Whatsapp, skype, or in person. The concept behind these humble endeavours is only to spread this divine subject and also to make the generations next to be ready for Astrology.

Many astrology students generally feel that the analysis presented in many books are lacking clarity or they are not sufficient enough for one to understand. It appears that some books have edited out the main crux of arriving at life events such as education, marriage, child birth, longevity, foreign travel, health or diseases, etc. Hence it is felt that the analytical method should be more of interactive form and should be simplified down to earth. An attempt is made in this book in that direction.

While writing this book, a special care is taken as to not to peep into the personal life of the persons consulted us, whose horoscope is discussed in any book. The author is sure that this book will make a good one for reading and learning Astrology.

This book is written in 3 volumes as of June 2020, and cover only Prashna Astrology based on time and Number charts. No natal charts are discussed.

The prashna horoscopes mentioned in this book are genuine, since the author is a practicing astrologer. The practice and records of 16 years of Astro-life is not reflecting in these books, since some of the data were lost, while some horoscopes were not stored. For all the horoscopes stored, the article is written for the benefit of the respected readers. However, some of the earlier books also cover the prashna articles.

If any of the esteemed readers who wish to contact the author, they are welcome.

With humble pranams to Almighty God!

Yours sincerely

# SOME FUNDAMENTAL RULES

In this write up, a sincere attempt is made to portray some of basic fundamental rules and stipulations that are required for covering the astrological concept passed on through this book:

## PLANETS

In traditional Astrology there are 09 planets or celestial bodies, which are considered for evaluation such as Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn, Rahu and Ketu, and they are called Surya, Chandra, Kuja, Budha, Guru, Shukra, Shani, Rahu and Ketu in India. While Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus and Saturn are planets, Sun and Moon are considered luminaries. Rahu (North Node) and Ketu (South Node) are the nodes of Moon (Chandra).

## SENS

There are 12 signs considered in the zodiac, which are called Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius and Pisces. They are called as Mesha, Vrishabha, Mithuna, Karkataka, Simha, Kanya, Tula, Vrischika, Dhanu, Makara, Kumbha and Meena in Indian terminology.

## SEN LORDS

Sun (Surya) rules Leo (Simha). Moon (Chandra) rules Cancer (Kataka). Mars (Kuja) rules Aries (Mesha) and Scorpio (Vrischika). Mercury (Budha) rules Gemini (Mithuna) and Virgo (Kanya). Jupiter (Guru) rules Sagittarius (Dhanus) and Pisces (Meena). Venus

---

(Shukra) rules Taurus (Vrishabha) and Libra (Tula). Saturn (Shani) rules Capricorn (Makara) and Aquarius (Kumbha).

## ASPECTS

All planets are having its dhrishti or view from its 07th position, which is called aspect. Whereas in addition to the 07th aspect, Mars (Kuja) aspect 04 and 08 from its position. Saturn aspects 03 and 10 houses, while Jupiter (Guru) aspects 05 and 09 houses. Influence of planets could be considered in a house where it aspects. Nodes (Rahu or Ketu) do not have aspect.

---

Native should be operating the dasha / bhukti / antara / Sookshma of particular planets, which have to be considered for selecting particular period for an event.

## RULTJG PLANETS

Ruling planets at the time of judgement of the event has to be considered. Ruling planets are:

1) Lagna sign lord
2) Lagna star lord.
3) Moon (Chandra) sign lord.
4) Moon (Chandra) star lord
5) Day lord.

In order to select a particular event, the ruling planets are taken into consideration at the time of judgement.

## LAGNA/MOON CHANDRA) SEN

Astrologers consider lagna or Moon (Chandra) sign (janma rashi) first house for counting the houses in a horoscope chart.

## NODES -RAHU/KETU

Rahu (North Node) or Ketu (South Node) are the Nodes and they are not planets. They are called shadowy planets in Vedic Astrology. Rahu (North Node) or Ketu (South Node) will give results of (a) planet conjoined with (b) Planet aspected by (c) result of its star lord (d) Sign lord in that order. In order to evaluate the significations of Nodes, let us check the following example chart:

In this example chart taken out at 7-49 a.m. at Hyderabad, India, on 17 Sept 2010, Iagna is rising in Virgo (Kanya). Rahu (North Node) is posited in $ 0 4^{th} $ house at 14 deg. Hence it is in the house of Jupiter (Guru) and is in the star of Purva Ashada ruled by Venus (Shukra). It is conjoined with Moon (Chandra). Hence Rahu (North Node) will give result in the following order:

---

VISWANATH NAIR

a) Planet conjoined with :Moon (Chandra).
b) Planet aspecting Rahu :None in this case.
c) Rahu posited in the star of :Venus (Shukra)
d) Rahu posited in the house of :Jupiter (Guru).

So, as per the rule above, Rahu (North Node) will give result of Moon (Chandra), Venus (Shukra) and Jupiter (Guru). In order words, wherever Moon (Chandra), Venus (Shukra) and Jupiter (Guru) give result, Rahu (North Node) will also offer the same result.

In the case of Ketu (South Node), it is posited in 10th house at 14 deg. in Arudra star ruled by Rahu (North Node) in Gemini (Mithuna). It is further aspected by Saturn (Shani). So, let us take the results of Ketu (South Node) in the following way:

a) Planet conjoined with :(None in this case)
b) Planet aspecting Ketu :Saturn
c) Ketu posited in the star of :Rahu
d) Ketu posited in the house of :Mercury.

Therefore, Ketu (South Node) will give result of Saturn (Shani), Rahu (North Node) and Mercury (Budha) i.e., wherever they offer the results, Ketu (South Node) just follows.

## PLANETS IN NODE STAR

The planets which are posited in the stars of Nodes, viz. Rahu (North Node) (Arudra, Swati, Satabhisha) and Ketu (South Node) (Ashwini, Makha, Moola) will offer the result such that wherever the Nodes offer.

# REMEDIAL MEASURES

As far as remedial measures are concerned, various schools of thoughts do prevail. But traditional astrology proclaims and advocates various types of remedial measures or antidote.

## Recovery

Recovery of disease is signified by signifying pianets of houses 01, 05 and 11. Saturn offers very slow or partial recovery If Saturn and Moon are linked disease could re-surface.

## Graha Shanti

Graha shanti or propitiation of a planet is done for each planet as per traditional astrological parlance. This should be done as per the advice of a priest.

## Mantra Japa

There are various mantras in vogue. One could chant maha mruthyunjaya mantra for recovery of iliness or recovery from accidents, etc.::

अस स्वमंकृ यजामहे
सुगानिं पुतिष्ठने।
उवार्ककमिति बन्धनान
मुत्योमोखित माड़मुतात।

23

---

Maha mruthyunjaya mantra can be used for longevity also. However the deeksha for chanting mantra should be obtained from a priest or a spiritual guru.

## Snaana

Snaana means taking dip in pious rivers. This can be undertaken as per the advice of a priest.

## Yantra / Thantra

Tantra is using thaavis for various mantra-thantra vidhya. It is a kavach or sachet from disease and promotes good health and recovery.

## Mahamruthunjaya yantra

Maha mruthunjaya yantra can be effectively used for recovery from illness, accident or to attain full longevity.

Shri Maha Mnityunjaya Yantra

24

---

## Dhanvanthri yantra

Dhanvanthri yantra could be used for recovery from diseases, etc.

## Aghora yantra

Aghora yantra coupled with Mahamruthunjaya yantra could be used for recovery from diseases, etc.

(IMAGES ARE NOT EXACT)

## NAVA DANYA VEDE REM ED ES)

Sun signifies wheat. Moon is for rice. Mars is for toor dal. Mercury is for moong dal. Venus is for Jupiter is for chana.

Ravi (Sun, Sunday): This is the day of Sun God. Sun is the symbol of fire. He likes brown colour and so his favourites include gur or jaggery, red pulses, kesar or saffron, chandan, wheat, red fruits and red sweetmeats. Appeasing Sun God with these items is supposed to give longevity and good health.

Som (Moon, Monday): Also called Chandrama, moon likes all things white so you can offer him rice, milk, curd, ghee, salt, white sweets, sugar, cashew nuts and radish. One becomes calm and cough-relating problems go away.

Mangal (Mars, Tuesday): Karthikeya is the lord of planet Mangal. His favourite food items include red masoor daal, jaggery, pomegranates and all other red fruits etc. One is said to be cured of headache and blood related complications

Budh (Mercury, Wednesday): Lord Budh likes green colour and so his favourite food items are green moong, torai, lauki, parwal, pumpkin, green fruits and green sweetmeats. He can help cure mental disorders.

Brihaspati (Jupiter, Thursday): Lord Brihaspati loves yellow items like yellow laddu, chana daal, jaggery and yellow fruits. Health benefits include healthy kidneys and disease-free life

---

Shukra (Venus, Friday): Shukravar is supposed to be the day of the God of ghouls and ogre. Shukracharya likes white-coloured items like rice, sugar, semolina and white fruits. He bestows happiness and material comforts on his devotees

Shani (Saturn, Saturday): Lord Shani loves all things black like black urad, mustard oil, gur, black sesame, black flowers, black fruits, black sweetmeats, black chana etc. If Shani is pleased, your enemies would automatically be destroyed. He cures all physical ailments.

Rahu-Ketu (Ascending and Descending Lunar nodes, respectively): Amongst all the Navgrahas, Rahu and Ketu are both supposed to be the shadow-grahas, meaning thereby, wherever one goes, the other also follows. They both move together. They like black items like black urad, black til and black sweets. Please Rahu-Ketu and remain healthy and wealthy forever.

Remedial measures mentioned in these books are as per the prescriptions of acharyas of Tantrik, Vedic, Lal Kitab method of remedies. They have to be put to practice Astrologers, and either author or Publishers do not take guarantee for its efficacy. Also, remedies do vary from horoscope to horoscope or issue to issue in a horoscope.

## RULING PLANETS RP

The lagna is at 22 deg. in Taurus, and this area is ruled by Venus and Moon. Moon is at 16 deg. in Taurus and this area is again ruled by Venus and Moon. Day lord and hora lord is Mars. Concluding, RP are Moon, Venus and Mars.

## ASTRO HUTS

For disease, you could judge houses 01, 06 and 08. Saturn can be taken for karaka for disease. $ 0 1^{s t} $ house is for self or body (shareer). $ 0 6^{t h} $ house is for disease or roga sthana. $ 0 8^{t h} $ house is un-expected events serious nature, etc.

---

## RP Vs QUERY

Ruling planets are Venus, Moon and Mars. Since Venus signifies 01, 06 and 08 houses, his query is tallied with the Ruling Planets (RP).

## ANALYSE

The dasha is that of Moon-Saturn. Moon signifies houses 01 and 03 only. Saturn signifies houses 04, 09 and 10 only. Hence these planets are not capable of causing disease.

Luckily, Mars, who signifies 05th house through Mercury, is aspecting lagna and dasha lord. 05th house can negate disease. Moreover, lagna lord Venus is in 11th house, which is house of gains. Houses 01 and 11 are found to be good houses, which can offer recovery. Hence it was indicated to the person that he is not suffering from any illness.

## DASHA

Then what is the issue? Since the period is that of Saturn-Moon, it is felt that he must be having cold.

The ruling planets, as mentioned above, are not connected to houses of disease, except Venus. Venus is occupying 11th house. Hence, the person need not worry. Jupiter signifies Houses 01, 06 and 08, but its star lord Venus is in 11th house. Also, luckily the dasha is not of either Venus or Jupiter.

Hence it was explained to the person who visited me not to worry now.

---

## RP LNK

The dasha period is that of Saturn-Moon. Ruling planets are Venus, Moon and Mars. Bhukti lord Moon is in RP, but Dasha lord Saturn signifies Sun and not found in RP.

## REMEDES

However, it was explained to the querient person you can chant maha mrithyunjaya mantra. You can visit Venkateshwara Swamy or Narasimha Swamy temple, since Mercury and Mars signify 05th house.

## RULTJG PLANETS

The lagna is at 17 deg. rising in Gemini, and this area is ruled by Mercury and Rahu. Moon is in Taurus (Venus) and it is in Kritika star, ruled by Sun. Day is Monday, which is ruled by Moon. Hora lord is also Saturn. The ruling planets are Mercury, Rahu, Venus, Sun and Moon. Hora lord is Saturn.

## ASTRO HNTS

Lagna lord is Mercury, who denotes business. Moon sign lord is Venus, who signifies $ 0 7^{th} $ house, which is for business. Moon is in $ 1 2^{th} $ house and hence his mind is on loss of business (12th house denotes loss.).

House of business is 07th house. 02nd house stands for money, while 11th house is for gains or profit. The person seeking the question (PSQ) has already invested money and is doing business. So he is expecting profit. Now the question is will he get profit or will he end up in loss?

## RP Vs QUERY

The ruling planets are Mercury, Rahu, Venus, Sun and Moon. RP Mercury is the planet of business. Rahu signifies Mercury, who is for business. Venus signifies 07 and 10 through Jupiter. 07th house is for business, while 10th house is for profession. Sun and Moon have exchanged their stars and Moon is in 12th house, which is for investment. Hence RP tallies with the nature of the query.

## ANALYSE

Jupiter signifies 07 and 10. 10th house is for profession. Rahu is in lagna and getting the aspect of Jupiter. So the person is interested in business. Moon is lord of 02nd house (Money) is in 12th house. Hence it indicates investment of money. Moon signifies 08th house through Sun. Hence the money is in the hands of others. Let us go to the DBA too.

## DASHA

The dasha is that of Sun-Mercury. Dasha lord Sun is in 08th house. 08th house denotes money of the opposite party. Sun is in the star Shravana, which is ruled by Moon. Moon is lord of 02nd house, but occupies 12th house and it is in the constellation Kritika, which is ruled by Sun. 12th house indicates loss. Hence 02nd lord Moon offers result of 08 and 12, which are detrimental houses for business from the point of return of money with profit.

In addition to the above, Saturn and Moon are occupying the stars of dasha lord Sun. Saturn is conjunct with Sun, who is in Moon star. These indicate punarphoo. Day lord is Moon, who is in 12th house and signifies 02 and 08 too. Hora lord is Saturn, who is in 08th house.

It was indicated to the person, who asked the question (PAQ) with regard to business, that the aspect of business shall not be good.

The natal chart is of this person is 19 Sep 1979 at 06-40 a.m., Latur, Maharashatra. We can apply the same dictum as expressed in the prashna chart for this natal chart too and see how business suits him.

Positive business aspects are seen from 02, 07, 10 and 11. Native can pray the devata or deity of the planets signifying these houses, according to the DBA running. For recovery of money, he can use yantra.

## RP LIJK

The dasha is that of Sun-Mercury. The ruling planets are Mercury, Rahu, Venus, Sun and Moon. Hora lord is Saturn.

Dasha lord Sun and Bhukti lord Mercury are in the RP.

---

Hence there is link established between DBA planets and RP.

## REMEDES

For successful running of the business, he can use maha sudarshana yantra, vyaapara yantra, etc. He should visit Vishnu temple on Fridays for business aspects.

As per Vedic system, PAQ can propitiate deities of planets signifying houses 02, 07, 10 and 11 for good business and profit.

## RULNG PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is in Taurus at 24 deg., and this area is ruled by Venus and Mars. Moon is 16 deg, in Capricorn, and this area is ruled by Saturn and Moon. Week day is Saturday, which is ruled by Saturn. Hence the ruling planets at the time of judgement of the kundli are Venus, Mars, Moon and Saturn.

## ASTRO HINTS

07th house is for marriage, partner, etc. 05th house is for love. 02nd house is for family. 11th house is for fulfillment of desires. If these houses are linked, then marital life shall be good. However, if 07th house is linked to houses 06 and 12 along with Saturn, then marital life may end in divorce.

## RPVsQUERY

Lagna star lord Mars occupies 07th house, which is for house of marriage. Lagna Venus is in the constellation Satabhisha, which is ruled by Rahu, who occupies 02nd house (family). Saturn is in the star of Sun, who signifies 04th house (home). Hence these planets signify nature of the query.

## ANALYSE

Mars, Rahu and Venus are the planets, who signify the 07th house.

It is agreed that Venus is lord of 06th house, while Mars is lord of 07 and 12. But Venus is not occupying 06th house. Mars is not occupying 12th house. Hence they offer lesser result of these houses. On the contrary, 07th lord Mars is in 07th house. It is placed in the constellation Jyeshta, which is ruled by Mercury, who signifies 02, 03, 05 and 09. Hence the native may not go in for divorce of the spouse, instead will try to accommodate another spouse.

## DASHA

Dasha is that of Moon-Saturn-Mercury-Saturn-Ketu-Rahu.

Dasha lord Moon is in own star in and is in 09th house. Bhukti and sookshma lord Saturn signifies houses 04 and 09. Antara lord Mercury is lord of 03 and 06, but occupies 09th house. Prana lord Ketu is own star in 08th house, but is conjoined with Jupiter, who signifies 08th house. Mars signifies houses 02 and 07. But Mars is not in DBA. However, Mars is aspecting Rahu, who is Deha lord. Indeed, Deha lord Rahu is in 02rd house and signifies houses 07,08,09, IT, etc.

These combinations do not cause disturbance in marital life.

## RP LINK

Dasha is that of Moon-Saturn-Mercury-Saturn-Ketu-Rahu. The ruling planets at the time of judgement of the kundli are Venus, Mar’s, Moon and Saturn. Dasha lord Moon is in RP. Bhukti lord and Sookshma lord Saturn is in RP. Antara lord is Mercury is not in RP, but Rahu is. Mars signifies Mercury too. Ketu is not in RP, but could be ignored. Hence DBA planets are linked to RP mostly.

## REMEDES

As per Vedic method, remedies can be done such as propitiation of deities of Mars or Mercury. Person can visit Venkateshwara swamy, Narasimha Swamy or Karthikeya temples and pray. Person can wear red coral gem stone. Person can wear red or green clothes. For marital harmony and happiness, specific yantra can be given.

For detailed analysis on various issues on marital life, esteemed readers could study book titled "Marriage, Child birth and Sub lord System" written by this author.

## RULNG PLANETS (RP)

## RP Vs QUERY

It is to be noted that the question was asked when hora lord and day lord signifies Mars, who is karaka for buildings. Hence the question is reflecting in the prashna chart.

## ASTRO HUTS

As far as home is concerned, it is for keeping family members. Hence 02nd house is to be considered. 02nd house also signifies money, without which, we cannot buy or construct a home.

04th house is for home. Mars is karaka planet. 12th house is for investment. 07th house is opposite party. 08th house is money of opposite party. 10th house is land and building of the opposite party. 06th house is for loan. 11th house is for gains. Jupiter is for Bank.

When there is a link between these bhaava and planets, one can buy home.

## ANALYSE

Planets, which signify pertinent bhaav, are as follows:

(a) 02nd bhaav - Mercury, Moon and Rahu.
(b) 04th bhaav - Sun and Saturn.
(c) 07th bhaav - Mars, Rahu, Ketu, Mercury.

(d) 06th bhaav - Venus, Jupiter, Rahu, Ketu, Mars and Mercury.

(e) 08th Bhaav - Jupiter, Mars, Rahu, Ketu, Sun and Mercury.

(f) 12th Bhaav - Mars, Rahu, Mercury and Ketu.
(g) 10th bhaav - Saturn, Venus, Mercury, Moon and Rahu.

---

However, students should riot forget a specific point, which is that Saturn signifies 04th house and also 04th lord Sun is in the constellation of Mars. Mars is karaka for land and buildings.

These planets signify pertinent houses and in their DBAS, they can make PAQ buy house.

## DASHA

Current VMD is Mercury-Jupiter-Jupiter until 10 Feb 2020. The next antara shall be of Saturn until 21 June 2020.

Out of the pertinent bhavas, Dasha lord Mercury signifies bhaav 02, 05, 07, 08, 10 and 12 through self and Rahu. Mercury is in the RP. The significations of these bhav are explained earlier. But it does not signify 04th house. Hence we need to wait until the pertinent period of a planet which signifies 04th house appears.

Bhukti lord is Jupiter. It is not in Ruling planets. But Jupiter is in Purva Ashada star, which is ruled by Venus. Venus is in RP. So Bhukti of Jupiter is considered.

Jupiter is Dhana Karaka and it signifies bhavas 01, 06, 08 and 11. Jupiter also does not signify 04th house. So, we can try for Antara of Saturn Antara of Saturn should be selected since Saturn signifies 04th house through Sun.

Indeed, Mercury-Jupiter-Satui n-Mai's period operates in May 2020, Person asking question (PAQ) can go in for home during this period. Sookshma of Mars is selected since it is karaka for building. Mars is in RP.

Since Saturn is involved, it could be a second-sale building. Since Mercury is involved, it could be apartment. Since Venus is connected through Saturn, it should be refurbished home. Since bhaavas 02, 06 and 11 are signified by Jupiter, who is karaka for finance, the person asking question (PAQ) shall get loan.

02nd lord Mercury is linked to Jupiter through Rahu. Hence the person shall be forced by Jupiter to go in for loan in its period.

Actually, the ruling planets (RP) are Venus, Mars, Mercury, Moon and Sun. Out of the pertinent bhavas Venus signifies 01 and 06. But Venus is not in RP. But Jupiter is in the star of Venus, viz. Purva Ashada. Hence Jupiter is considered instead of Venus. Moon is in the star of Mercury in Ashlesha in Cancer (Kark).

Since Saturn and Moon are in mutual aspect, PAQ shall be forced to negotiate more than one home. This is because of the fact that the link between Saturn and Moon causes punarphoo, which some astrologers call as "vish yoga". This is also called phunarphoo, which interprets as "repeat". This means that one proposal shall come close to finalization, but it can get cancelled. The second such proposal shall be better than the previous one.

As far as remedies are concerned, since 06, 08 and 12 are not linked to 04th bhava, i.e., the planets ruling 04th bhava viz. Sun and Saturn, there are no disputes expected in with the property. Hence there may not be serious remedies required. In order to get the purchase transactions smooth, the native can visit Subramanya (Kartikeya) temple on Satudays.

For all vastu related issues, one can use Vastu yantra. This will ward off any issues. Signification of Saturn in 04th house can cause some cold behaviour, lack of peace of mind, paucity of happiness & food, etc. at home. Since Ketu is not linked, presence of negative energy is ruled out. Hence major remedies are not prescribed for this PAQ.

## RP LINK

The current dasha is that of Mercury-Jupiter-Jupiter. RP are Venus, Mars, Mercury, Moon and Sun. Dasha lord Mercury is in RP. Bhukti and Antara lord Jupiter is not in RP. But Rahu denotes both Jupiter and Mercury.

## REMEDIES

04th lord Sun is in the star of Mars. Mars is karaka for land or building. The person can pray lord Karthikeya on Sundays or during Sun hora.

For buying home, the person can wear yantra. Person can use brown and red colour clothes. Person can wear red coral gem stone.

## REFERENCE

If the esteemed readers want to read more articles on home purchasing, they are welcome to read the book titled "An astrological treatise on Case Studies" written by this author.

## RULING PLANETS

The lagna is at 08 deg. in Virgo, and this area is ruled by Mercury and Sun. Moon is at 02 deg. in Aquarius, and this area is ruled by Saturn and Mars. Day lord is Sun. Hora lord is Venus. Hence the ruling planets at the time of judgement of the kundli are Mercury, Sun, Mars and Saturn. Hora lord is Venus.

## ASTRO HINTS

04th house along with Sun denotes heart. Some Astrologers do consider Moon too, probably due to its lordship over Cancer sign (Kark Rashi), being 04th house from Aries (Mesha).

Lagna is for self. 06th house is for disease. Saturn is the karaka or causative ptanet fpr disease. 08th house is for sudden events like serious nature, etc. Mars stands for attack. Recovery should be deciphered from houses 01, 05 or 11.

## RPVs QUERY

RP Mercury signifies 06th house (illness). Sun is in 06th house and denotes heart. Mars is occupant of 04th house, which is for heart. Saturn is planet of disease and it is in 06th house (roga). Further, Saturn is in the star of Sun (heart). Hora lord is Venus signifies 05th house through Jupiter, which is for recovery. Hence the RP tallies with the nature of the disease.

## ANALYSIS

The prashna chart is drawn for the time of reporting of hospitalization of the person. As said earlier, the prashna lagna is flipped to 12th house, which is the
lagna of the grandmother (father's mother).

Lagna lord Sun is in 06th house, which is for disease.
Sun is karaka for heart. It is in the star of Moon and it
conjunct with Saturn in 06th house. Hence function of
the heart is under threat and is the person is
hospitalized. Mars is in 04th house (heart) and it is in
the constellation Jyeshta, which is ruled by Mercury.
Mercury is in 06th house (illness)

12th lord (Hospitalization) Moon is in the constellation,
Dhanishta, which is ruled by Mars (04, 06).

## DASHA

However, dasha is; that of Mars-Ketu-Jupiter-
Jupiter. It is felt that the person should recover, if the
person concerned is father's mother. This is because
Bhukti, Antara and SoOkshmalord is Ketu and Jupiter,
respectively, who occupy 05th house and are not linked
to 06 or 08 or 12th house.

Accordingly, a message was sent and reply came two
days later stating that the grand-mother is recovered.

## RP LINK

Dasha is that of Mars-Ketu-Jupiter-Jupiter. Ruling
planets are Mercury, Sun, Mars and Saturn. Hora lord
is Venus.

Dasha lord Mars is in the RP. Bhukti lord Ketu or
Antara & sookshma lord are not in RP. But Jupiter is in
the star of Venus, who is hora lord. Hence this way there is a link assured between DBA planets and RP.
REMEDIES

For this type of issues, maha mrithyunjaya homa,
yantra, etc. are better remedies than any other.

It was widely reported in media that Mr. VG Siddhartha, who is a businessman in Bangalore had gone missing. This person is the son-in-law of a former Chief Minister.

A prashna chart was drawn as illustrated above, and the findings are explained below:

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is at 20 deg. in Leo, and this area is ruled by Sun and Venus. Moon is at 01 deg. in Gemini, and this area is ruled by Mercury and Mars. Week day is Monday, which is ruled by Moon (Chandra), who is also the hora lord.

Concluding, RP are Sun, Moon.

Venus, Mercury, Mars and

---

## ASTRO HINTS

If there is a link between houses 01, 08, badhaka and 12 along with planets like Saturn, Mars and Rahu, then it indicates suicide. There could be a direct link between houses 08 and 12. Also there should be a link between Moon and Mercury. $12^{th}$ house is for sacrifice, moksha, etc. $08^{th}$ house is for longevity.

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Sun is in $12^{th}$ house (moksha, sacrifice). RP Venus is also in $12^{th}$ house. Mars and Moon signify badhaka house. Hora lord is Jupiter, who signifies 08 and 12 along with a watery sign, viz. Scorpio.

Lagna lord Sun is in $12^{th}$ house, which is for moksha. Sun is in Cancer sign, which signifies river. RP indicate the nature of query.

## ANALYSIS

Rahu signifies Mercury, Moon and Jupiter. Mercury denotes 12 and 08. Moon denotes Mars and houses 09 (badhaka) and 12 (Moksha). Jupiter denotes 08 and 12. $08^{th}$ house is for longevity; $09^{th}$ house is badhaka and $12^{th}$ house is for moksha, sacrifice, etc.

Mercury is not linked to Moon. However, Moon is conjunct with Rahu. So Rahu denotes both Mercury and Moon. Hence the native got into several mental stress, fickle mindedness, etc.

---

Saturn is in 05th house, but it is in the star Purva Ashada, which is ruled by Venus. Venus is in 12th house. So Saturn signifies 12th house too. Saturn is in mutual aspect with Moon, who is in Mars star Mrigashira. Moon is conjunct with Rahu. Rahu signifies Saturn, Moon and Jupiter. Jupiter denotes houses 08 and 12. Saturn signifies 07, which is a marak house. Rahu is very detrimental and signifies Jupiter, Mercury, Moon and Saturn. Rahu is in sudden death, when in 12th house.

## DASHA

DBAS are Mars-Mercury-Rahu-Saturn-Venus-Mercury. Mars is badhaka. Mercury is for moksha. Jupiter is in Mercury's star, while Jupiter signifies 08 and 12. Mercury is retrograde. It signifies 08 and 12. Hence it is felt that the native ctoes not care about his life and longevity.

Saturn signifies houses 05^ 0% 08, 10 and 12 through self and Venus. Venus is in turn signifies the same houses. All the 09 planets do signify 12th house viz., Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn, Rahu and Ketu. 12th house is for loss. 05th house is for gain of the opposite party (07). Planets placed in 12th house and their link with Saturn robs one's sleep. 08th house is opposite party's money. Jupiter, Ketu, Rahu and Mercury signify 08th house. Jupiter is in 04th house and it is in retrograde motion. 04th lord is Mars, who is in 12th house and its star lord Mercury is in retrograde motion. This robs his peace of mind. Ketu's connection with 07th house would have caused disappearance of people with whom he dealt. Rahu's connection with 07th house would have caused cheating by persons whom he dealt with. Houses 05, 08 and 12 are not favourable for monetary gains in business. Rahu's link with 07th house and 10th house through Saturn, involving dual signs should result in various malpractices in business and profession.

A message was sent in group forum stating that it is a suicide case and the body should be in water (Cancer sign), since lagna lord is in Cancer. It is also a fact that many planets do occupy watery signs.

## RP LINK

DBAS are Mars-Mercury-Rahu-Saturn-Venus-Mercury. The ruling planets at the time of judgement of the kundli are Sun, Venus, Mercury, Mars and Moon.

Dasha lord Mars is in the RP. Bhukti and Deha lord Mercury is the RP. Antara lord Rahu signifies Jupiter, Saturn, Mercury and Moon. Mercury is in the RP. Sookshma lord Saturn is not in RP, but it is in the star of Venus, who is in RP. Prana lord Venus is in RP. Hence there is link between DBA planets and RP.

## REMEDIES

There are various remedial measures adopted for recovery of money and also for avoiding sacrificing own life though un-natural mode. Since in this case the unfortunate event has already taken place, no remedy is possible.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is in Aries at 01 deg., and this area is ruled by Mars and Ketu. Moon is at 06 deg. in Leo, and this area is ruled by Sun and Ketu. Day lord and hora lord is Mars. Culminating, RP are Mars, Ketu and Sun.

## ASTRO HINTS

Our home is $ 04^{th} $ house. Sale should be seen from $ 03^{rd} $ house, since it is vyaya or loss to $ 04^{th} $ house. Opposite party is $ 07^{th} $ house. Money of opposite party should be $ 08^{th} $ house. Our money is in $ 02^{nd} $ house. Mars is karaka for building. When we sell our home $ 10^{th} $ house gets activated, since it becomes home for opposite party.

---

When we sell our property (02nd house), the ownership is transferred (08th house) to the opposite party (07th house).

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Mars is karaka for land and buildings. Mars is hora lord also. The question is about sale of home. Mars is karaka. It is lord of lagna. Lagna is in Ashwini star, which is ruled by Ketu, the person wants to get rid of his home. Moon is in Ketu star, being lord of 04th house (home). Since it is sale, Ketu star is good, who is mokshakaraka. Since the ruling planets are linked to Mars, 04th house, 07th house, etc. it is felt that the question is genuine and the prashna horoscope indicates it.

## ANALYSIS

Lagna is in Aries. Moon tn Ketu star Makha. Mars is the karaka for land and buildings. It is lord of the lagna, which indicates self,.It is placed in Jupiter star Visakha in 07th house. This will indicate opposite party.

Mars is lord of 08th house and thus it indicates money of the opposite party. But 10th house should also be linked, indicating home for the opposite party. 10th house is governed by Saturn.

Since this link is not appearing, it was felt that Mars, who is karaka for home, should get linked to 10th house through Saturn. In the prashna horoscope, Mars is in Visakha star in Libra. So Mars should travel to Anuradha star. At this time, Mars shall be in 08th house and also shall signify 10th house.

Hence it was predicted to him that when Mars reaches Anuradha star, he will be able to sell his home.

---

Anuradha star is ruled by Saturn. Also at which time Mars shall indicate home and money of the opposite party.

Accordingly, the author received a recorded message on 02 Jan 2020 from this person stating that one person came and gave advance on 01 Jan 2020 at 13- 30 hours in Hyderabad. On this day, Mars was in the star of Saturn, viz. Anuradha in Scorpio.

At this time the DBA as per prashna horoscope was of Ketu-Rahu-Mercury-Saturn-Rahu-Mars. Dasha lord Ketu denotes 09th house. It is conjunct with Sun, Saturn and Jupiter. Sun and Moon is in Ketu star. ## ASTRO-HINTS

$ 0 2^{n d} $ house denotes money of self. $ 1 1^{t h} $ house is for gains. $ 0 7^{t h} $ house is opposite party. $ 0 8^{t h} $ house is the money of the opposite party. $ 0 6^{t h} $ house is loss to the opposite party, being $ 1 2^{t h} $ to $ 0 7^{t h} $ house. ## RP Vs QUERY

RP Jupiter signifies 08th house through Venus, which is money of client. Mercury signifies 07th house (client). Ketu signifies Saturn houses 06, 10 & 11, which are loss of client, profession and gains, respectively. Hence the RP indicates nature of query.

## ANALYSIS & DASHA

In this horary (Prashna) horoscope, the lagna is in Pisces.

(a) 11th house is signified by Saturn, Sun, Mercury, Mars, Rahu, Ketu, Venus and Moon.

(b) 02nd house is signified by Mars, Rahu and Venus.

(c) 07th house is signified by Mercury, Mars, Rahu and Venus.

Antara lord is Saturn. Saturn signifies 06 and 11. There are no planets in the star of Saturn.

Sookshma lord is Mercury. Mercury signifies 07 and 11. Hence it was felt that the opposite party should send the money the same day itself.

Dasha is that of Ketu-Rahu-Saturn-Mercury-Mercury. The Ruling planets are Jupiter, Mercury and Ketu.

## RP Vs QUERY

## ANALYSIS-MARRIAGE

Rahu and Ketu signify 01, 02, 03, 04, 07, 08, 10 and 12. Rahu is in own star, but signifies Mercury, Sun, Mars, Jupiter and Saturn.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is at 00-29 deg. in Leo, and this area is ruled by Ketu and Sun. Moon is at 18 deg. in Capricorn, and this area is ruled by Saturn and Moon. Day lord is Saturn. Hora lord is Mercury. Culminating, the RP are Sun, Ketu, Saturn and Moon. Hora lord is Mercury.

## ASTRO-HINTS & ANALYSIS

Houses for marriage are considered 02, 07 and 11. 02nd house is for family and addition of a member. 07th house is for spouse, partner, etc. 11th house is for fulfillment of desires.

One shall not get married at all if 07th house is weak and linked to houses 06 and 12 along with Saturn, Ketu and barren sign. Barren sign is Gemini, Leo and Virgo. 06th house is negating 07th house, while 12th house is for loss, separation, relinquishing, etc. If this rule is not present in the chart, the person shall get married.

Among the RP planets Sun, Saturn, Moon and Mercury are occupying 06th house. 06th house is negating 07th house, which is for marriage. All these planets do signify 12th house too.

Saturn is the only planet from RP, who signifies 07th house. But it is occupying 06th house and its star lord Sun is also in 06th house and is denoting 12th house.

However, Venus signifies 03, 07, 10, 11 and it is in the constellation Satabhisha, which is ruled by Rahu. Jupiter is in the star of Venus in 05th house and is aspecting Rahu. Hence these planets are good for marriage. But these planets are not in RP.

---

Dasha is that of Moon, who signifies 06 and 12 only. Hence it was predicted that the time for marriage has not come. PAQ need to wait and do the remedial measures.

## RP Vs QUERY / RP LINK TO DBAS

The RP are Sun (lagna lord) is in 06th house and it is placed in the star of 12th lord Moon. Ketu signifies 05 and 07 through Jupiter. Saturn and Moon do signify house 06 and 12. Hora lord is Mercury too signifies houses 06 and 12. Hence the nature of query is indicated by RP planets. Dasha is that of Moon-Mercury. These planets of course, signify houses 06 and 12 too.

## REMEDIES

For getting married, one can use vivaha yantra. One can visit Shiva temple on Fridays and pray during evening hours. One can offer flowers to Goddess Parvati.

## LAGNA OR ASC

Just looked at my wrist watch and judged that the lagna is in Aries (Mesha). How?

The date of judgement was 16 March, which means Sun shall be around 01° in Pisces (Meena). Indeed, at the time of judgement, Sun was at 01°-45' in Pisces. Calculating around two hours for one lagna, which is Pisces at the time of Sun rise, and another two hours for Aries (Mesha) lagna, I calculated that the lagna should be in Aries, since the time is around 9-30 a.m.

Indeed the Sun rise was at 06-27 a.m. in Hyderabad. This means that the longitude of Sun and lagna shall be the equal and same:

06-27 a.m. Sun Longitude : Pisces 01 deg.38 min
06-27 a.m. Lagna Longitude : Pisces 01 deg.37 min

08-01 a.m. Lagna Longitutfe' Aries 01 deg.37 min
08-01 a.m. Sun Longitude : Pisces 01 deg.41 min
09-30 a.m. Lagna Longitude : Aries 26 deg.31 min.
09-30 a.m. Sun Longitude : Pisces 01 deg.45 min.

Hence it was concluded that the lagna should around the fag end of Aries.

Planetary positions were known to the author, including Moon star lord. Students should have a glance at the planetary positions on a day-to-day basis.

01) You will suffer from Blood pressure.

Blood pressure is indicated by a combination of lagna and 08th house along with Mars, Moon and Jupiter. A combination between Moon and Mars indicate liquid in red colour, which shall be blood. 08th house indicates underground. Blood flows in the blood vessels. Hence blood is deciphered from 08th house. Jupiter causes pressure or large scale. Hence there could be pressure of blood when these combinations combine in a horoscope.

## 02) You are married from other caste after a love affair.

Moon is the karaka of the mind. It is in 07th house, which indicates that the person mind is disposed to his spouse. This does not mean he marries a girl from outside his caste. Here Moon is in the star of Rahu, viz. Swati, while Rahu occupies 05th house, which is for love. Rahu is for other caste. Hence it was conceived that the person married from outside his caste.

## 03) You shall be going abroad, which shall be temporary.

For going abroad, one should see houses 03, 09 and 12. Lagna is in Aries or Mesha. 03rd house is in Gemini (Mithuna). 09th house is Sagittarius (Dhanu) and 12th house is in Pisces (Meena). These houses are ruled by Mercury and Jupiter. Indeed, both Mercury and Jupiter are in mutual aspect. Jupiter is in the star of Mars, who signifies lagna (self). Special point (Punya saham is in 09th house.

He immediately told the author that he is indeed travelling abroad on the same week itself.

## 04) You have thoughts beyond your control.

Since Rahu signifies Ketu along with 05th house, this was told him that his thoughts are beyond 360°.

---

## 05) You suffer from anxiety.

Since lagna and 05th house are linked, some time, he will have anxiety too.

## 06) You shall be out of the family.

02nd house denotes family. 04th house is for home.
02nd lord is Venus, who is in 12th house (unknown area), and it is in the star of Saturn (long distance).
04th lord is Moon, who is in Rahu star Swati in Libra (Tula), but it is in Rahu star. Rahu is in turn is in Ketu star. Hence it was predicted that you shall be away from family and home.

## 07) You are quite selfless person.

If somehow lagna is linked to Ketu or Ketu is linked to lagna, it is said that the person shall be selfless. Here in this horoscope, the lagna lord Mars is in Ketu's star Ashwini in lagna itself. Hence this reading was given to the person.

## 08) You will earn in Lakhs per month.

In a given scenario, if Jupiter signifies 11th house, one can earn in Lakhs. In this kundli, Jupiter is in 06th house, which is also considered a very good bhaava for earnings, since this becomes an artha house. Jupiter is in Mars star Chitra, while Mars signifies 11th house through Ketu. Hence this reading was given to the person.

---

09) You have some hearing, understanding and calculating issues.

Mercury is located in 12th house in a watery sign, viz. Pisces, while it is lord of 03rd house (hearing). Hence person shall be hearing, memory, and understanding issues when the period or sub period of Mercury or a planet placed in Mercury star operates.

## RP LINK

In this kundli neither RP nor DBA were considered.

## REMEDIES FOR BLOOD PRESSURE

Walk and exercise regularly. Exercise is one of the best things you can do to lower high blood pressure, Reduce your sodium in-take (salt). Salt intake is high around the world, Eat more potassium-rich foods like banana, avacada, Orange, Melon, Apricot, Leafy vegetables, tomoto, milk, yogurt (curd), etc. Cut back on caffeine, Learn to manage stress; Eat dark chocolate or cocoa; Lose weight; Never drink alcohol.

As per Vedic system of remedies, since Sun is 05th house lord, person can pray Lord Karthikeya for recovery on Sundays. The person did not ask for remedies. Indeed, person did not even know that the author is an Astrologer.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is in Cancer at 01 deg., and this area is ruled by Moon and Jupiter. Moon is also in Cancer sign at 19 deg., and this area is ruled by Moon and Mercury. Day lord and hora lord is Venus. Concluding, the RP shall be Moon, Jupiter, Mercury and Venus.

## ASTRO HINTS

If there is link between houses 01, 03, 08, 12 along with Rahu, Saturn, Mars and Sun, one shall be in jail. Jail is signified by Rahu and 12th house.

01st house is for self. 03rd house negates 04th house (home). 08th house along with Saturn is for restriction. Mars signifies police while Sun is for Government. Jail is supervised by Police and isowned by Sun.

## BACKGROUND

There is an Indian national lurking in Pakistan Jail by name Kulbhushan Jhadav. It is reported that he is a retired Naval Officer. A question was asked by somebody that whether he will be released from jail.

## RP Vs QUERY

The lagna is in Jupiter star Punarvasu. Lagna lord is Moon, who is in constellation of Mercury. Both Jupiter and Mercury signify houses 01 and 03. RP Venus is in 12th house and gets aspect of 08th lord Saturn. So RP indicates nature of query.

---

## ANALYSIS

Rahu is in 12th house and it is conjunct with 04th lord Venus. It is conjunct with Sun also, who is for Government. Rahu is further under aspect from Saturn, who is 08th lord. Rahu also signifies Mercury, who is in lagna. Hence Rahu signifies 01, 03, 08 and 12 along with Sun and Saturn. Mars is conjunct with Mercury (12) in lagna and it signifies 08th house too.

## DASHA

The VMD at the time of horary chart is that of Mercury-Venus-Rahu-Jupiter.

Dasha lord Mercury is in the lagna. It is lord of houses 01 and 03. It is placed in Pushya star, which is ruled by Saturn. Hence Mercury signifies houses 01, 03, 06, 07, 08 and 12 by self and through Saturn. Saturn is retrograde, which indicates situation is out of control.

Bhukti lord is Venus. Venus is lord 04 and 11 and is placed in 12th house. It is placed in Ardra star, which is ruled by Rahu. Hence Venus offers the result of 04, 11 and 12 along with the significations of Rahu. 04th house is for home, but Venus is in 12th house (isolation).

Antara lord Rahu, being a Node, is signifying Saturn, Jupiter, Sun, Mercury and Venus. Rahu is conjunct with Sun and Venus. It is getting the aspect of Saturn. It is placed in the constellation Punarvasu, which is ruled by sookshma dasha lord Jupiter.

Finally, Rahu is in Gemini (Mithuna), which is ruled by Mercury. Both Sun and Venus are in Rahu star, and occupies 12th house.

---

These permutations and combinations indicate that the person is in Jail. Since Jupiter signifies houses 01, 03, 09 and 12, person should be abroad.

Since Sun, Venus and Rahu are connected to 01 and 03, he could have been injected with poisonous chemicals while in custody.

Venus bhukti in Mercury main period is until Apr 2022 and next bhukti is of Sun until Feb 2023. Both these periods do not favour release from jail, since they are in Rahu star in 12th house and are aspected by retrograde Saturn.

Hence, it is concluded that as per this prashna chart, the person may not be put out of jail.

## RP LINK

The VMD at the time of horary chart is that of Mercury-Venus-Rahu-jupiter. The RP shall be Moon, Jupiter, Mercury, and Venus. Dasha lord Mercury, Bhukti lord Venus and Sookshma lord Jupiter are in RP. Antara lord Rahu denotes Jupiter and Mercury, who are in RP. Hence there is link between DBA planets and RP.

## REMEDIES

There is specific yantra, which can be given to the person, if he is allowed to wear, for getting out of the jail. But in this case, it may not be possible, since the person is languishing in foreign jail. But we can do certain pooja and homa remedies.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

(a) Lagna is in Virgo (Kanya), which is ruled by Mercury (Budha). The lagna is at 01 deg., which is corresponding to Uthar Phalguni star in Virgo, which is ruled by Sun (Soorya).

(b) Moon (Chandra) is in Capricorn, which is ruled by Saturn (Shani). Moon is in the constellation Dhanishta, which is ruled by Mars (Mangal).

(c) Week day is Thursday, Which is ruled by Jupiter (Guru). Hora lord is also Sun.

Concluding, the RP shall be Mercury, Sun, Saturn, Mars and Jupiter. *

## ASTRO HINTS

If 08th house is weak, and if it is linked to 01, 06, 08, 12 and Badhaka along with Saturn, Mars, Rahu and Sun, it shall be sure that one can be put to death by Government. Saturn is for punishment and it is marana karaka or causative planet for death.

Mars, Saturn and Rahu indicate killing. Sun is for government. 01st house is for self. 06th house is for injury. 08th house is for longevity. Badhaka is for death. 12th house is for punishment.

## RPVs QUERY

RP Mercury is signifies lagna (self) and 08 (longevity). Sun is for Government. It is in 09th house, being lord of 12 and this indicates that the person is connected to foreign land. Saturn is for restriction and signifies 06 and 08 through Ketu. Mars is 08th lord. Venus signifies badhaka. Hence RP indicate the nature of query.

## ANALYSIS

08th house is in Aries (Mesha). It is ruled by Mars. Moon and Ketu are in the star of Mars. Ketu is in 06th house. Moon is in 05th house. Badhaka lord is Jupiter (07th house). It is not signifying 08th house. It is in Moon star in lagna and offers the result of 01, 05 and 11. Hence Jupiter is not considered dangerous.

Venus is in badhaka house. Venus is in Mercury star. So Venus signifies 01, 07 and 08 involving lagna, badhak and ashtama. So Venus could be dangerous. But it has to get connected to Saturn, mars and Rahu along with houses 06, 08 12 and bahdaka.

But 08th house has to become weak. Here 08th lord is Mars. It is in 09th house, which is not badhaka. So, 09th house shall function as Punya house. Also, Mars is in own star Mrigashira. Hence Mars is safe. It is not linked to either Mercury or Venus. Planets signify 12th house are Sun and Rahu. They are not linked to Badhaka or 08th house.

Hence it was concluded from this prashna horoscope that the person shall not be awarded death penalty by an enemy country.

## DASHA

Dasha is that of Mars. Mars is of course lord of the 08th house. But it is in 09th house and is in own star. Hence it is strong. Hence the dasha lord is not dangerous to award penalty.

---

## RP LINK

Dasha is that of Mars. The RP shall be Mercury, Sun, Saturn, Mars and Venus. In this kundli, specific dasha is not elaborately looked into. However, dasha lord Mars is in RP.

## REMEDIES

## ASTRO HINTS

At the time of the query, dasha is that of Rahu-Moon. Bhukti of Moon ends towards Dec. 2019.

By this time, the transit Saturn shall move to Capricorn and thereby its aspect on the 10th house shall cease to function. Aspect of Saturn on 10th house is not good for speeding up promotion. ## ASTRO HINTS

Lagna signifies self. 07th house shall be opposite party. Success is deciphered from 06, 10 and 11. If planets signifying 06, 10 and 11 are stronger from lagna, self will win. If planets signifying 06, 10 and 11 are stronger from 07th house, opposite party will win. Jupiter gives good marks. Mars is for success. Houses 03, 08 and 12 indicate lower status along with Saturn. Ketu indicates ejection from the scene or lack of support. 04th house is for stagnation. 05th house is for Politics. Saturn is for Democracy. Houses 05, 08 and 12 also signify favour for opposite side.

## RP Vs QUERY

Lagna star lord is Moon, who signifies 10th and 11th houses, which are for success. Moon signifies 11th house through Mars. 11th house is for Assembly, Parliament, Senate, etc. Sign lord of Moon is lord of 05 and 10 and it is in constellation Uthara Ashada, which is ruled by Sun. Sun signifies politics, while 05th house is for politics. Day lord is Saturn. Saturn is for Democracy. Hence the chart indicates the nature of the query.

## ANALYSIS

In this case, Capricorn (Makara) is the lagna. The impeachment motion against President Trump was brought in by Opposition Democratic Party under leadership of Speaker Nancy Pelosi. Hence Capricorn shall be lagna for Democrats and Cancer (Kark) shall be the lagna of Trump.

Lagna is in Capricorn (Makara) and 10th house shall be Libra. 06th house shall be Gemini (Mithuna). 11th house shall be Scorpio (Vrishchik).

Rahu is occupant of 06th house, but is linked to Jupiter, Saturn and Sun, who are in 12th house. Hence Rahu may not be useful for Democrats for this motion.

Mars and Moon are occupying 10th house, which is comparatively good, especially when Moon signifies Mars. Mars is also lord of 11th house. However, Mars is occupying the star Visakha, which is ruled by Jupiter, who is in 12th house. Hence Mars offers more result of houses 10 and 12, which may not be so favourable.

Jupiter is in 12th house and it is in the constellation Moola, which is ruled by Ketu. Ketu itself is in own star in 12th house. Hence Jupiter may not be useful for Democrats for this motion.

Venus signifies Sun and also is lord of houses 05 and 10. But its star lord Sun is in 12th house. Sun is in Ketu star, while Ketu itself is in own star in 12th house. Hence the author gets the feeling that in all the steps that the success of the motion is looks bleak.

## ROTATED CHART

In order to see the success of opposite party, viz. President Trump, the chart is rotated to 07th house.

## ASTRO HINTS

02nd house denotes nature of speech. 03rd house signifies what one communicates. 04th house is mind. 05th house is for thoughts. Rahu is for fraud.

Sun and Jupiter are truth sayers. Mercury is diplomatic. Venus is deceptive, but lovely.

Mercury signifies expression, while Jupiter signifies impression, Saturn shows some deceptiveness and do not communicate fully. Ketu is careless or shows lack of interest.

Moon is innocent, but its star lord shall prevail. Mars is for "one cut - two pieces" attitude such as sudden rush, anger, etc.

However, all in all, the star lord of the planet will prevail.

## RP Vs QUERY

Lagna is in Saturn sign and Jupiter star. Jupiter is lord of 02nd house, which is for speech. Moon is in Cancer sign, which is her own sign.

Moon signifies mind of the person. Moon is in Jupiter star Punarvasu, while Jupiter is 02nd lord.

Day lord is Saturn, who is in Sun star, while Sun signifies 04th house (mind). Hence the RP is tallying with the question raised. Hence the Prashna horoscope is correct and can be analyzed.

---

## ANALYSIS

Lagna is rising in Aquarius. 02nd house shall be in Pisces or Meena rashi. 03rd house shall be in Aries or Mesha rashi. 04th house shall be in Taurus or Vrishabha. 05th house shall be in Gemini or Mithuna.

02nd house (speech) is signified by Jupiter and Moon. Moon is brittle like glass, so it can be manipulated. But it is in the constellation Punarvasu, which is ruled by Jupiter. Jupiter is truth saying person. Hence Mostly person should speak truth too. But Rahu also is signifying 02nd house, which is negative. Rahu is getting the aspect of Mars too. Hence there could be bluffing too by the person when the period or sub period of either Rahu or Mars operates.

02nd house (earnings) is also Signifying wealth. Since Mars and Rahu are signifying 02nd house, one shall have unscrupulous earnings during their period.

03rd house (communications) is signified by Mars, Venus and Rahu. Mars may communicate bluntly. Venus shall have some deceptive nature and it is in the star of Mars.

04th house (mind) is signified by Venus, Sun, Jupiter, Ketu and Rahu. Sun and Jupiter indicate straight mind, but strict. Ketu is casual and moody. Venus and Rahu are deceptive. The mind of Venus cannot be observed from outside.

Rahu and Mercury are signifying 05th house (intention), which indicates that the person may be having thoughts of gambling type.

---

## DASHA

Dasha at the time of judgement of chart is Jupiter-Rahu-Rahu-Saturn-Rahu-Rahu.

In the Dasha array Jupiter shall be good for speaking truth. But Rahu is appearing four times along with Saturn. Hence for the moment, what the person is speaking is not correct and there could be lies, misdirecting words, etc. Saturn denotes a state where not all words are spoken. Saturn is like abstract, but not pure and sincere like Jupiter.

One boy at the next door of the author attended exam and arrived at the author's home and sat down, since he did not have keys of his flat. The author asked him if he had issues with examination papers, especially Mathematics, to which the boy said "you are right,sir".

## RULING PLANETS

Lagna is at 06 deg. in Cancer, and this area is ruled by Moon and Saturn. Moon is at 16 deg. in Capricorn, and this area is ruled by Saturn and Moon. Day lord is Jupiter. Hora lord Saturn. Thus RP are Saturn, Moon and Jupiter. Hora lord is Saturn.

For study and examinations, Astrologers consider houses 09, 06 and 11 or houses 04, 06 and 11. Jupiter and Mercury are considered planets of education. Saturn and Ketu show failures.

Houses 03, 05, 08 and 12 do work negatively for education. 04th house is for basic education. 09th house is for higher education. 06th house is loss to opposite party. Hence it is considered for competitive exams.

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Saturn is in 06th house (competition) and is in the star of Ketu, who is in 08th house (disappointment). Jupiter is lord of 06 and 09 indicating competition and higher education. Jupiter is planet of education. Hence some of the RP indicates nature of query.

## ANALYSIS

Among the karaka planets, Mercury is in retrograde motion, which means that the subjects related to Mercury (Budha) should have some difficult propositions.

Mercury denotes mathematics in general. 03rd house signifies accounting. Its lord Mercury is placed in Ketu star Ashwini in Aries (Mesha), whereas Ketu is placed in 08th house.

Mercury itself signifies 03 and 12. Hence it is felt that the boy is not so good in Mathematics. Further, Ketu is getting the aspect of Saturn.

---

However, what about his studies? Planets signifying 04 and 09 are Venus and Jupiter. Jupiter also signifies 01 (self) and 06th houses (competition). Jupiter is karaka for education. It is aspecting Venus too. Although Mercury is having issues, it is strong in a fiery sign (Aries).

Jupiter is in the star of Moon, while Venus is in the star of Saturn. Hence in any of the dasha of Jupiter, Venus, Moon or Saturn, the boy should study well.

## DASHA

The dasha at the time of judgement was that of Moon-Saturn-Ketu.

Dasha lord Moon is not linked to either houses of 04 or 09, but Jupiter (lord of houses 06 and 09) is in Moon star. Hence during Moon dasha, the boy will study under assistance from Jupiter.

Since Jupiter signifies these houses and it is in the star of manas karaka (causative planet for mind) Moon, the boy is interested in studies.

The boy is having dasha of Moon-Saturn, at the time of Judgement. This can make him re-appear in couple of examinations. Saturn is in Ketu star. But Venus is in 09th house, being lord of 04th house and it is the star of Saturn.

Next bhukti is of Mercury, which is karaka for education. It gives certain set backs, but in a fiery sign, which could eventually favour the boy.

---

Lagna lord is Moon, who is dasha lord. Moon is in own star in Capricorn, which is 07th house. 07th house is for opposite party.

Moon is in own star, which means it offers the result of female. Hence the boy should have friends of female nativity.

The person had to re-write some examinations, which include Mathematics.

## RP LINK

The dasha at the time of judgement was that of Moon-Saturn-Ketu. The ruling planets are Saturn, Moon and Jupiter. Hora lord is Saturn.

Dasha lord Moon is in RP.Bhukti lord Saturn is in RP. Antara lord Ketu is getting the aspect of RP Saturn. There is link between DBA planets and RP.

## REMEDIES

The person can wear green clothes or keep green ribbon in his pocket. Person can visit Venkateshwara Swamy temple on Wednesdays. He can visit Maha Vishnu temple also. He can wear vidya yantra.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

The lagna is at 15 deg. in Cancer sign, and this area is ruled by Moon and Saturn. Moon is at 26 deg. in Cancer, and this area is ruled by Moon and Mercury. Day lord and hora lord is Moon. Culminating, the RP are Moon, Saturn and Mercury.

## ASTRO HINTS

Sun signifies brown colour. Moon signifies white colour Mars signifies deep red colour. Mercury signifies green colour. Jupiter signifies yellow colour. Venus signifies variegated colour.

---

Some astrologers say Venus signifies bright white color too. Saturn signifies black or blue colour. It is said that Saturn signifies blue colour during day time while black colour during night time. Rahu signifies honey (Gomedh) colour. Ketu signifies grey (ash) colour.

A vehicle is signified by 04th house and Venus (Vahana). Mercury or dual signs signify four-wheeled vehicles. Jupiter signifies heavy vehicles. Saturn is for carrying like material transportation (labour). 03rd house denotes travel, short journeys, etc.

Owning anything is signified by 02nd house (sampat). 12th house is for investment (vyaya). 05th house is one's love.

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Moon signifies Mercury (03 and duality). Dual houses can indicate four wheeled vehicle. Saturn signifies 04 and Venus. Saturn is in a dual sign. Hence four-wheeled vehicle is indicated. Hence some of the RP indicates nature of query.

## ANALYSIS

In this Prashna horoscope, Moon is in the constellation of Mercury, who denotes 03 (transport) and 12 (investment). Lagna star lord is Saturn. Saturn signifies Venus and 04th house. Venus and 04th house is for Vehicle.

Mars is lord of 05th house. So Mars indicates his love. It is in 04th house (vehicle). Mars is in Libra, which is a sign ruled by Venus (vehicle). Mars denotes red colour. Hence the author asked PAQ if he likes to go in for red colour vehicle, to which he said "YES".

Planets who are signifying the pertinent houses for buying vehicle are such that 02nd house is for money. 03rd house is for travelling. 04th house is for vehicle. 06th house is for rent. 11th house is for gains. 12th house is for investment.

Jupiter signifies houses 02, 04, 06 and 11. It is in the constellation Moola, which is ruled by Ketu. But Ketu denotes Venus, who lord of 04th house.

Venus is karaka for vehicle. Hence Jupiter can help person to buy a vehicle.

At the time of judgement of Prashna horoscope, the dasha is that of Mercury-Jupiter-Mercury-Venus. Dasha and Antara lord Mercury signifies houses 03, and 12 which are for short journey and investment, respectively. Bhukti lord Jupiter signifies 02, 04, 06 and 11. Indeed Rahu signifies all the pertinent houses and it also signifies Jupiter and Mercury.

Hence it was predicted to the PAQ that the period is favourable for buying a vehicle and he may go ahead.

Since 06th house is also included, the vehicle should be placed for rent. If houses 02, 04, 06, 07, 08 along with Venus are linked, one can rent out the vehicle, and get rent money. 02nd house is for money. 04th house is for vehicle. 06th house is for rent. 07th house is for opposite party. 08th house is money of opposite party.

---

Jupiter signifies houses 02, 04, 06, 07, 08 and it is linked to Venus through Ketu. Jupiter is not in the RP. But it is in the star of Ketu.

Ketu signifies, Venus, Jupiter, Saturn and Sun. Sun is signifies houses 02 and 06. Saturn signifies houses 04, 06, 07 and 08. Saturn is in the RP. Hence the period favours buying of vehicle and letting it out on rent.

## RP LINK

Dasha is that of Mercury-Jupiter-Mercury-Venus. The RP are Moon, Saturn and Mercury. Dasha and Antara lord is Mercury, who is in RP. Bhukti lord is Jupiter, who is not in RP. But It is conjunct with Ketu and Saturn. Hence Ketu offers result of both Jupiter and Saturn. Sookshma lord is Venus, who is not in RP, but Saturn is in RP, who is in Venus star.

## REMEDIES

Since the PAQ is Christian by belief, asked him to light 7 candles in his Church on Saturdays.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is at 24 deg. in Aries, and this area is ruled by Mars and Venus. Moon is at 20 deg. in Leo, and this area is ruled by Sun and Venus. Day lord is Venus and hora lord is Saturn, Culminating RP are Mars, Venus, Sun and Saturn. Hora lord is Saturn.

## ASTRO HINTS

Sun signifies brown colour. Moon signifies white colour. Mars signifies deep red colour. Mercury signifies green colour. Jupiter signifies yellow colour. Venus signifies variegated colour. ## RP Vs QUERY

## ANALYSIS

While on visit to a cloth store, the relatives were trying to buy a saree for an elderly woman, the author told his niece who was standing by, that her mother will select blue-brown combination of saree.

There was no horoscope available. The author just looked at the watch and confirmed lagna in Venus star Bharani in Aries. The planetary positions were known.

Lagna is in Bharani star in Aries. Moon is in Venus star Purva Phalguni in Leo. Both stars are signified by Venus. Venus is in the star of Mars, who is lagna lord. Hence the RP tallies the question in mind of the author. Venus signifies clothes, since it is lord of the 02nd house from Aries.

The lagna is Aries at that time and Sun was in Anuradha star. Sun is 05th lord. 05th house shall be your liking. Sun is in Saturn star Anuradha in Scorpio. Sun signifies brown colour, while Saturn signifies blue. Saturn signifies both blue and black.

---

But the author gets the feeling that Saturn signifies black colour during night time, while blue colour during day time. In normal circumstances, people do not buy black saree. The reading came correct. The relatives bought a blue saree with brown designs printed on it.

It is only God who is great. Gurus teach us this subject. Application is the discretion of the student.

## RP LINK & REMEDIES

Since this reading was given based on the time without a horoscope, there was no dasha or RP were considered. There is no specific remedies need.

## BACKGROUND

This prashna was done by one the students of this Author, who run a driving school. One lady came to learn driving under this student. This student is so intelligent that he took the time of arrival of the lady. He looked at his wrist watch and determined lagna and position of all planets. He then asked that lady that if her marriage in divorce, to which she said "yes".

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is at 26 deg. in Libra, and this area is ruled by Venus and Jupiter. Moon is at 12 deg. in Pisces, and this area is ruled by Jupiter and Saturn. Day lord and hora is Saturn. Culminating, RP are Venus, Jupiter and Saturn.

---

## ASTRO HINTS

01st house is for self. 07th house denotes marriage, partner, etc. 08th house shall be Mangalya. 12th house is for bed comforts. 05th house is for love. 02nd house is for family and money. 11th house is for fulfillment of desires. 06th house is for enmity. If 06, 07 and 12 along with Saturn are linked, marriage will end in divorce.

## RPVs QUERY

RP Venus signifies houses 01 and 02 which are for self and family. Venus is karaka for marriage. Jupiter is 06th lord, which is negating 07th house. Saturn is in the star of Venus. Moon is in the star of Saturn in 06th house. RP indicates nature of query.

## ANALYSIS

07th lord Mars is in 12th house in Virgo (Kanya) and it is in the constellation of Mars itself. This can cause lack of satisfaction, irritation, quarrel, etc.

Mars is being aspected by Moon from 06th house, viz. Pisces. Moon is in the star of Saturn, viz. Uthara Bhadra. Since Moon is in 06th house, it can cause enmity in marital life, since it is aspecting 07th lord.

Further, since Moon is in the constellation of Saturn, Moon offers the nature of Saturn. Saturn is the karaka planet for enmity. 06th house negates 07th house (vyaya). Hence there is strong enmity in marital life.

Further, Saturn is aspecting Mars by the 10th house aspect. Hence Saturn further adds enmity. Since 07th house has become weak and it is linked to 06, 12 and

---

Saturn, it was conveyed to that person that your marriage is divorced.

## RP LINK

In this Prashna horoscope, no specific Dasha or Ruling planets were considered to give such a simple reading.

## REMEDIES

There are no specific remedies provided.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is at 10 deg. in Scorpio and this area is ruled by Mars and Saturn. Moon is at 05 deg. in Pisces and this area is. ruled by Jupiter and Saturn. Day lord is Moon. Hora lord is Saturn. Culminating, the RP are Mars, Saturn, Jupiter and Moon.

## ASTRO HINTS

09th house signifies long distance travel. Venus is for vehicle. Airy sign is good for vehicle travelling by air. Mercury is for airy vehicles. 12th house is for stay in unknown place.

However, the horoscope should not have link between badhka and house of longevity (08th). This can cause some issues such as vehicle could get damaged or so. If there is a link between 01, 04, 06, 08 and badhaka along with Saturn, Mars and Rahu, it could be possible that there shall be vehicular accident.

## RP Vs QUERY

Lagna is in Scorpio, which is ruled by Mars. Mars is posited in 09th house, which is for long distance. Lagna star lord is Saturn. Saturn is in star of Venus, which signifies vehicle in general. Venus is in an airy sign, which indicates air borne vehicle. Moon is also in the star of Saturn. The sign lord of Moon is Jupiter, who also denotes Mercury and 09th house. Hence the prashna chart indicates the nature of the query.

---

## ANALYSIS

Lagna is signified by Mars, Jupiter, Venus, Rahu and Ketu.

Planets signifying 04th house (vehicle) are Saturn, Sun, Mercury, Moon, Rahu and Ketu. Saturn and Ketu are in the star of Venus viz., Purva Ashada. Saturn and Venus are in mutual aspect. They are involved with Rahu and Ketu. All these planets signify 02 (maraca) and 08th (accidents). But for accidents, Mars has to come into picture.

06th house is signified by Mars only. 08th house shall be signified by Mercury, Mars, Jupiter, Rahu, Ketu, Venus, Saturn, etc.

09th house shall be Badhaka for all Sthira lagna (fixed Ascending signs). Planets signifying 09th house are Moon, Sun, Mercury, Mars, Jupiter, Rahu and Ketu.

Jupiter denoting lagna, maraca and badhaka; Mercury signifying 08 and badhaka; and Saturn signifying maraca, 03rd house and 08th house are all in retrograde mode (vakri), which can cause the situation out of control.

Mars signify lagna, 06th house, 08th house and badhaka. It is also conjoined with Mercury. It is aspecting 04th house (vehicle).

## DASHA

The dasha at the time of launch of the Vehicle was Saturn-Mercury-Mercury-Moon-Saturn(Shani- Budha-Chandra). As per this prashna chart, whenever there is a period of Saturn, Venus, Jupiter or Mercury operates, Rahu also comes into picture.

## PRASHNA RULES

Lagna, Lagna star lord, Moon or day lord should signify the event which we are looking for. Otherwise, Astrologers can verify the DBAS in that respect. For offering a prediction, Astrologers may depend upon the ruling planets / DBAS.

## RULING PLANETS

a) Lagna is in Gemini (Mithuna), which is ruled by Mercury (Budha). The lagna is at 22 deg., which is corresponding to Punarvasu star in Gemini, which is ruled by Jupiter (Guru).

b) Moon is in Scorpio, which is ruled by Mars (Mangal). Moon is in the constellation Jyeshta, which is ruled by Mercury (Budha).

c) Week day is Friday, which is ruled by Venus (Shukra). Hora lord is Mars.

The RP are Mercury, Jupiter, Mars and Venus.

Lagna is in Punarvasu star in Gemini (Mithuna), which is an airy sign. Jupiter is in Scorpio (Vrishchika), which is ruled by Mars and is in the star Jyeshta, which is ruled by Mercury. Mercury is for flights. Scorpio is 06th house from lagna (Ascendant).

## ASTRO HINTS

If houses 04, 06, 08, 12, badhaka along with Saturn, Venus, Rahu and Mars are connected, there could be vehicle accidents. Airy sign (Vayu tatwa rashi) denotes any activity in the air.

---

## ANALYSIS

Gemini is an airy sign, which indicates any activity in the air. Lagna star lord is Jupiter, who is in Mercury star. Jupiter is maraca and badhaka lord. Moon signifies Mercury, who is for flight like objects. Venus signifies Vehicle in general. Mars signifies accidents. It is in star of Venus in Purva Phalguni. Hence the ruling planets tally with the prashna horoscope such as vehicle accident. Moon is also in the constellation Jyesta.

Both Jupiter and Moon are under aspect from Mars. Mars denotes accidents. Jupiter signifies houses 01, 03, 04, 06 and badhaka.

Saturn is placed in 02nd house from Moon sign, which indicates Moon is in saade-saathi (Elarta Shani). Saturn is placed in Venus star Purva Ashada in maraca and badhaka house in Sagittarius (Dhanu). Saturn is lord of 08th house (accident) and is linked to Rahu and Ketu. Rahu signifies Saturn, Mercury and Jupiter. Ketu signifies Saturn, Jupiter and Venus.

Mars is lord of 06th house and it is conjunct with Venus and Mercury. Venus is for vehicle in general, while Mercury is for airy objects like flight, etc. Mercury lord of lagna and 04th house, but is not placed in an airy sign, Rahu who is agent of Mercury, being in Gemini. So Rahu offers these results.

Hence 01, 04, 06, 08, badhaka along with Mars, Mercury, Venus, Saturn and Rahu have been formed.

---

## DASHA

The dasha lords are Mercury-Saturn-Mercury-Jupiter-Mars-Rahu. Hora lord was Mars. The significations and nature of these planets are discussed above.

So the prashna chart at the time of accident is indicated in the prashna horoscope.

## RP LINK

At the time of crash-land of the vehicle, the dasha lords are Mercury-Saturn-Mercury-Jupiter-Mars-Rahu.

The RP of the crash-land time kundli are Mercury, Jupiter, Mars and Venus.

Dasha and Antara lord is Mercury, who is in RP. Bhukti lord is Saturn is in the star of Venus, who is in RP. Sookshma lord is Jupiter, who is in RP Mercury star. Prana lord is Mars, who is in RP. Deha lord is Rahu, who signifies both Mercury and Jupiter. Hence there is link of DBA planets with that of RP.

## REMEDIES

For this mundane case, there are no specific remedies.

## RULING PLANETS

## ASTRO-HINTS

04th house signifies land. 08th house signifies hole Mars is for hole. Scorpio is considered natural 08th house, while Cancer signifies land, since it is the natural 04th house. 11th house is for fulfillment of desires.

---

Moon signifies water. Barren land should not favour water. Barren signs are Gemini, Leo and Virgo. Watery signs are good. Jupiter signifies sweet. Venus-Saturn should offer chemical waters. Venus alone should signify sour type water. Moon alone should signify salty water. Sun governs the pungent taste. Mars governs the bitter taste. Mercury governs a mixed taste. Saturn governs the astringent taste (e.g. plantain, pomegranate).

As far as color of water is concerned, Sun is for brown; Moon is for white; Mars for red; Mercury for green; Jupiter for yellow; Venus mixed colour or white colour; Saturn for black colour; Rahu is for honey colour and Ketu is for ash colour. But all these colours may not be pertinent for water.

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Jupiter is in the star of Node Ketu, who in turn signifies Venus (08). 08th house is for deep, secret, hole, etc. Mercury is in Scorpio and it is a hidden sign. Saturn too signifies 08th house through Venus. Hence RP is tallied with nature of the query.

04th house is in Gemini, which is a barren sign. Barren signs may not contribute for procuration of water. But 04th lord is Mercury, who is in a watery sign, viz. Scorpio. Scorpio may have some contamination of water.

Mercury is in the star of Saturn, viz. Anuradha. So water may not be plenty. But Saturn signifies 11th house and so is Mercury.

---

Hence it was informed to the student that there shall be water in the bore well, but may not be so satisfactory.

## RULING PLANETS

Lagna is t 26-20 deg. in Sagittarius, and this area is ruled by Jupiter and Venus. Moon is at 05 deg. in Leo, and this area is ruled by Sun and Ketu. Day lord is Venus. Hora lord is Jupiter. Culminating the RP are Jupiter, Venus, Sun and Ketu.

Lagna lord is Jupiter. Hora lord is Jupiter. Jupiter is the karaka or causative planet for liver. Lagna star lord is Venus, who is lord of $ 0 6^{th} $ house, which is for roga or disease. Venus is placed in $ 0 8^{th} $ house, which indicates seriousness of the disease. Venus is in the constellation Pushya, which is ruled by Saturn. Saturn is roga karaka and placed in lagna (body).

Moon is in the constellation Makha, which is ruled by Ketu. Ketu signifies 01, 06, 08 and 12 along with planets Saturn, Jupiter and Venus. So, the ruling planets (RP) indicate that the person is suffering from serious,illness.

## ASTRO-HINTS

A student came to the author, and asked if her neighbor, who is suffering from liver disorder, can get liver transplantation. This is because the Doctors advised the patient for going for liver transplantation.

Liver is signified by Jupiter. 05th house is the place where liver is located. Disease is signified by 06th house and serious nature of disease should be deciphered from 08th house with the link of 06th house.

Tj,...

Lagna is for body or self. Saturn is karaka for disease. Rahu denotes cirrhosis, etc. 12th house is for hospitalization when linked to houses 01, 06 and 08.

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Jupiter denotes liver. Venus signifies 11th house, which is for liver of the opposite party. Hence these RP planets indicate the nature of query.

## ANALYSIS

Saturn is in the star of Venus in lagna, and thus signifies houses 01, 06, 08. But Venus is not 05th lord. Ketu signifies Jupiter, Venus and Saturn. Thus Ketu offers result of houses 01, 06 and 08. Indeed, dasha is that of Ketu only. Rahu is in the star of Jupiter, viz.

---

Punarvasu. It is linked to Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn. Saturn is in the constellation of Venus (Purva Ashada). Hence Rahu offers result of 01; 06, 08 and 12. So, the issue is serious and can warrant hospitalization. Liver is located in 05th house. 05th house is signified by Mars. Mars is not linked in the above permutations, except that Jupiter is aspecting Mars. However, from Moon sign, Jupiter signifies 05th house.

## DASHA

However, the question was asked when the DBAS of Ketu-Mars-Saturn-Mars-Venus was operating. In the array of DBAS, Mars is appearing. Hence it is confirmed that the person is suffering from liver disorder. Since Rahu is involved in the scope and with Jupiter, the disease could be liver cirrhosis.

## TRANSPLANTATION

Actually, the question was whether the person shall get transplantation of liver.

Lagna is the patient, who is the person in question (PAQ). 07th house shall be the opposite party or the donor. Liver is deciphered from 05th house. So donor's 05th house shall be the place where his liver is located. This shall be 11th houses for the PAQ. Removal is 12th house. 06th house for PAQ and Saturn should signify removal. So, houses 07, 11, 06 and Saturn should act for removal. Mars is for cutting or chopping. Liver has to be removed by cutting.

In this prashna kundli, no planet is signifying Mars. But if Mars is appearing in DBAS should help the person for cutting such as surgery, etc. Mars is appearing in DBAS, which is consolation for the PAQ. Indeed 08th house is for surgery along with Mars. Mars is in 08th house being lord of 05 (liver) and 12th (removal) houses. 11th house is for fulfillment of desires too.

Rahu signifies 01, 06, 07, 08, 11 and 12 through Mercury, Jupiter and Saturn. Whenever a period or sub period of Mercury, Jupiter or Saturn operates, Rahu too gets activated. Hence it was predicted to the student to tell the PAQ that he will get liver transplantation.

It was later learnt from the student that PAQ got his liver transplanted, and his is doing fine.

## RP LINK

The DBAS of Ketu-Mars-Saturn-Mars-Venus The RP are Jupiter, Venus, Sun and Ketu.

Dasha lord Ketu and Prana lord Venus are directly in the RP. Bhukti and sookshma lord Mars is getting the aspect of Jupiter, who is in RP. Antara lord Saturn is not in RP, but its star lord Venus is in RP. Hence we can see the link between DBA planets and RP.

## REMEDIES

Person can visit and pray Maha Vishnu or Venkateshwara temple on Thursdays or Jupiter hora or when Moon transits in the stars of Jupiter. Person can wear yellow sapphire gem stone. Person can fast on Thursdays.
