## LIVER

05th house and Jupiter (Guru) denotes liver. Jupiter is signifying 05th house through Mercury, since Jupiter is in Jyeshta star. Jupiter is in retrograde motion. But Jupiter is not linked to 06th house, but it is lord of 08th house.

Rahu is in Gemini (Mithuna) and it signifies Jupiter, Saturn, Mercury and Jupiter. Hence houses 01, 05, 06, 08 and 12 are the pertinent houses for liver related issues. Rahu signifies Jupiter and Saturn too. Saturn is in the star of Venus. This indicates there is liver cirrhosis to the person in question.

## DASHA

The dasha is that of Moon-Venus-Venus-Moon-Ketu.

Dasha and Sookshma lord Moon is in badhaka house and Saturn is in 12th house to the Moon. Venus is Bhukti and Antara lord and it is signifying houses 01, 06 and 12. Person shall be in serious conditions with disease and be hospitalized.

Prana lord is Ketu, who signifies Jupiter, Saturn, Mars and Venus. Jupiter signifies liver.

Though Jupiter is in retrograde mode, since it signifies 01 and 05th houses, it is not very serious. But Rahu denotes Jupiter too. Mars signifies 05th house through Rahu. 05th house is the place where liver is located.

---

Saturn (08 and badhaka) signifies Venus (01, 06 and 12). Saturn is in retrograde motion. Saturn is aspecting Rahu. Hence the disease is quite serious and out of control. On seeing all these combinations, the author informed his friend that the person may not survive.

Unfortunately, the person passed away when the Moon was transiting in Ashwini star in Aries.

## RP LINK

The dasha is that of Moon-Venus-Venus-Moon-Ketu. RP are Venus, Mars, Saturn and Moon. Venus is prominent in dasha as bhukti and Antara lord, who is in RP. Dasha Moon and sookshma lord Moon is in RP. Prana lord Ketu is not in RP, but it linked to Saturn, who is in the star of Venus, Indeed, Ketu is also in the star of Venus.

## REMEDIES

Mahamruthyun jaya homa and yantra are the only solution known to us for this kind of issue.

One lady reported to the author that her wallet is lost. She was asking if it was stolen when she was travelling in city bus.

Scorpio indicates 08th house from Aries, which is money of opposite party. Moon signifies 02, 07, 09 and 12. These houses indicate money, opposite party, hand of opposite party and loss, respectively. Hence the nature of the question is tallied as per the horary horoscope.

## ASTRO-HINTS

Lagna is for self. 02nd house Is for money. 07th house is opposite party or the person stealing the money (as per this case). 08th house is money of opposite party or loss to self. 05th house is gain of the opposite party. 03rd house is for hands.

09th house shall be right hand of the thief. 12th house is loss to self. 06th house is loss to opposite party. Moon and Venus denote female with average age. Mars, Rahu and Saturn indicate nature of stealing.

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Mars indicates 08th house, which is money for opposite party (thief here). Saturn signifies 02nd house (money). Venus signifies 07 and 12 denoting thief and loss. RP indicates nature of query.

---

## ANALYSIS

Planets signifying these pertinent houses are as per the table illustrated above:

Esteemed readers can see that Rahu is signifying all the pertinent houses. Rahu is conjunct with Mars and is receiving the aspect of Saturn and Moon. Rahu is for fraud. Mars indicates courage and adventure. When they are linked, adventure in the form of fraud is possible.

This indicates it is lifting of the money by someone. Saturn and Moon are in Venus star. Thus Rahu is involved with houses 02, 06, 07, 08, 09 and 12. This indicates that the money is stolen by a woman. Venus and Moon signify 09th house too, which is the right hand of the thief.

Hence thief used her right hand to lift the money, Rahu signifies 07 (opposite party), 08 (money), 02 (money of the person in question).

## DASHA

The dasha as per the horary chart is that of Venus-Moon-Mars-Mercury-Jupiter-Mercury.

Dasha lord Venus signifies 02, 07, 09 and 12, while bhukti lord Moon is signifying 02 and 09 by self. Indeed, both Venus and Moon have exchanged their star. Hence both signify houses 02, 07, 09 and 12. The - significations of these houses are explained earlier.

Antara lord is Mars, which is for adventure and courage. Mars denotes houses 01, 02, 05, 06, 08 through self and Jupiter. Sookshma and Deha lord is

---

Mercury, who signifies 01, 02, 05, 08 and 11 by self and through Jupiter.

Venus signifies beauty. Since 07th house is signified by Venus, who is in Moon star Rohini and that Moon is in Venus star Purva Ashada, the opposite party should be a woman. Moon signifies mother. Rahu, Mercury and Mars denote Muslims. The person who stolen the wallet should be a good looking married Muslim lady.

## RP LINK

Dasha is of Venus - Moon - Mars - Mercury - Jupiter - Mercury. The RP are Mars, Saturn, Jupiter and Venus.

Dasha lord Venus is in RP. Bhukti lord Moon is not in RP, but it is in the star of Venus. Antara lord Mars is in RP. Sookshma and Deha lord Mercury is not in RP, but it is in the star of Jupiter. Prana lord Jupiter is in RP.

the author few questions

## RP Vs QUERY

RP Mars signifies- 10th house (profession) through Saturn. Ketu signifies houses 07 and 12 through Jupiter. Moon signifies 02nd house (money). Venus is 07th lord (marriage). Mercury is 06th lord (service). Hence RP indicates nature of queries such as marriage and job.

## JOB IN 20 DAYS

For a paid job, one could consider houses 06 (daily duty) and 10 (profession). 02nd house is for money. 11th house is for gains.

Planets signifying 06 are Mercury, Rahu and Ketu. Planets signifying 10th house are Saturn, Mars, Venus Mercury, Ketu, Sun Jupiter and Rahu. The common planets for these two houses are Mercury and the Nodes (Chaaya graha).

So these planets do signify job for her. Dasha is that of Moon-Venus-Jupiter-Saturn-Mars. Moon signifies 02nd house. Jupiter, Saturn, Venus and Mars are appearing in 10th house. Venus is bhukti lord. 06th lord Mercury is in Venus star.

So she will get job in the same bhukti of Venus. Antara lord is Jupiter, who signifies 10th house. Sookshma lord is Saturn, who also signifies houses 10 and 11.

Prana lord is Mars, who signifies 10th house. Mars is in RP like other planets. If Jupiter is not in RP, it does not matter. Jupiter is in the star of Venus. Hence it was indicated to her she will get job immediately within this month itself. The author has asked her to search for the job.

## ELDEST IN THE FAMILY

Since lagna lord, which denotes self or PAQ, is in the constellation Anuradha, which is ruled by lithe lord Saturn, it was predicted to her that she is the eldest among the siblings. 11th house signifies elder co-born. Here lagna lord itself signified 11th house. Hence the PAQ should be the eldest in the family.

Mars is appearing in the RP and it is in the constellation Anuradha, which is ruled by Saturn.

## LOVE MARRIAGE

05th house signifies love. 07th house is for marriage. 08th house is for mangalya and 12th house is for sleep pleasures or shayana sukha.

Sun is the 05th lord. It is in star Purva Ashada, which is ruled by 07th lord Venus. So Sun offers result of houses 05 and 07. Hence there is a love angle in the life of this girl. Venus is in the star of Mars, who signifies 08. Jupiter is the 12th lord. 08th house is for mangalya and 12th house is for bed comforts.

Jupiter is in the star of Venus. Ketu and Rahu are linked to Saturn, Jupiter and Sun. These planets denote 05, 07, 09, 11, while Venus signifies 07, 08, 10 and 01 through self and through Mars. Venus is appearing in the RP and Sun is in the star of Venus. Venus signifies Mars (01 and 08).

So now the lagna is Libra or Tula. 05th house (Aquarius or Kumbha) shall be ruled by Saturn, Sun, Rahu and Ketu. 07th house (Aries or Mesha) shall be ruled by Mars, Venus, Rahu and Ketu.

05th house is for children. 05th house is ruled by Sun, Saturn, Rahu and Ketu. Sun is in Venus (female) star. So being male, Sun becomes female. Saturn is in Sun's star. So Saturn being eunuch, tender to become female through Sun. Rahu is in own star, so being eunuch, it cannot offer any gender. Ketu is in Venus star, which is female.

Venus is in Mars star Dhanishta. Mars is male. But Mars itself is in star of Saturn. Saturn is female in this chart (though originally eunuch).

Day lord is Mercury, who is in Venus star. Hora lord is Sun, who is female now as per this chart. Moon is in own star in Venus sign. Lagna lord is Mars, who is male, but is in Eunuch Saturn's star. Lagna star lord is Ketu, who signifies female through Venus.

All these permutations and combinations tend to tell the author to the girl client that she will be blessed with female kid.

## MOON

Since Moon, the karaka of mind, is in own star and that it is aspected by Mars (courage), the girl shall have a premeditated and courageous mind. Since Mars is aspecting lagna and Moon, she shall be courageous. And be disposed to spouse, since Mars is in 07th house. Since Moon signifies lagna and 03rd house, she shall like to travel and be courageous.

These houses indicate, self, mangalya, father, home of spouse and fulfillment of desires. 06th house is for daily service. 06th lord Mercury is in bhukti lord Venus star Purva Ashada.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

## BACKGROUND

The person called was married 10 years back and is unable to see a child in his home. He was calling the author to seek what are the methods to be adopted to have children such as IVF, Surrogacy or Adoption.

## ASTRO-HINTS

## Adoption

If houses 05, 06, 11 along with Mercury and Jupiter are linked, the PAQ can adopt a kid. 05th house is for child. 06th house is rent or adoption. 11th house is for fulfillment of desires. Jupiter is putra karaka and Mercury is for maternal uncle.

## RPVs QUERY

Lagna, Moon and Lagna star are connected to Rahu, which apparently indicate towards IVF. Hence the query is tallied.

## Analysis

Rahu is in lagna and it is in own star and hence it signifies Mercury. Further, it is aspected by Saturn, Mars and Jupiter. Hence Rahu offers the result of all these planets. On the other hand, Moon and Venus are in the constellation of Rahu, viz. Swati and Satabhisha, respectively.

Moon and Venus offers the result of Rahu along with that of Saturn, Jupiter and Mars. Planets may signify many houses. We need to take pertinent houses only.

Mars signifies 06, 07, 08 and 11. Jupiter signifies houses 05, 07 and 09. Saturn signifies houses 07 and 08. Mars does look good, but it does not signify 05th house. But Moon occupying 05th house and offers the result of Mars through Rahu. So Moon offers houses 05, 06, and 11. Since Rahu offers result of Mercury, so is Moon. Rahu also offers result of Jupiter. So is Moon. Hence Moon looks good for adoption.

At the time of judgement, the dasha is that of Rahu-Venus-Sun-Saturn-Venus.

## IVF

If Rahu is somehow connected 05, 07, 08, 11 along with Sun, one can go in for IVF technique. 05th house is for children. 07th house is external genital. 08th house is for internal genitals, while 11th house is for fulfillment of desires.

Rahu signifies Saturn, who is in the star of Sun, viz. Uthara Ashada. Hence Moon is again good here offering result of Rahu, while Rahu in turn offers result of Saturn, who is in the star of Sun. Saturn signifies houses 07, 08 and Sun. Mars signifies 11, while Moon signifies 05. Hence IVF is also favourable.

At the time of judgement, the dasha is that of Rahu-Venus-Sun-Saturn-Venus.

## Surrogacy

Houses 07, 08, 09, 03 and 04 along with Mercury and Jupiter should pave way for surrogacy.

01st house denotes self. 07th house is for partner. 08th house is for internal genitals like ovaries and uterus. 09th house is for third person. 04th house shall be 08th from 09th house, which stands for internal genital of the 03rd person. 03rd house shall be 07th from 09th house, which shall be the internal genital of the 3rd person.

As seen in the above, Moon and Venus do signify these houses through Rahu.

## DASHA

At the time of judgement, the dasha is that of Rahu-Venus-Sun-Saturn-Venus.

It was finally told to the PAQ that the couple should go in for IVF method for giving birth to child.

## RP LINK

The dasha is that of Rahu-Venus-Sun-Saturn-Venus. RP are Mercury, Rahu, Venus and Saturn. Dasha lord Rahu is in RP. Bhukti and Prana lord is Venus, who is also in RP. Antara and sookshma lords are Sun and Saturn, respectively. Saturn is in the star of Sun and is in RP. Hence DBA planets are linked to RP.

## REMEDIES

Venus, Jupiter and Moon are primary significators of 05th house. They are well placed in the prashna horoscope. Hence propitiation for the deities of these planets may be done, as per Vedic system. They can opt for Santana yantra, as per Tantrik system.

## ASTRO-HINTS

For Politics, we should take house 05 and planet Sun. $ 1 0 ^ { \mathrm{th}} $ house is karma sthan oft success. For successful competition, houses 06 and 11 are to be considered.

Houses 05, 08 and 12 are not favourable. Self is lagna, while $ 0 7^{th} $ house is for opposite party. $ 1 0^{th} $ house is for success of us, while $ 0 4^{th} $ house is house of success of opposite party. Jupiter is for more marks or runs or score. Saturn is for low marks and is also for democracy. We should consider $ 0 9^{th} $ house, because it signifies Kingdom or Federal, etc.

## RP Vs QUERY

05th house denotes Politics. Sun, karaka for Politics, is in 05th house. Lagna lord Moon gets the aspect of Sun. The day lord is Mercury, who occupies 05th house. It is in the star of Saturn, who denotes democracy, working class, etc. Saturn is lagna star lord. Lagna lord is

---

Moon, who is in 11th house, which is for Assembly, etc. Hence the prashna kundli tallies with the question in mind.

## ANALYSIS

The lagna is rising in Cancer (Kark Rashi), which is the lagna for the ruling AAP. Main opposition is BJP, since the Congress party is not seen much in the picture. The lagna of BJP is Capricorn (Makara rashi).

So, 10th house for AAP is Aries (Mesha rashi), while 10th house for BJP is Libra (Tula rashi).

Planets signify Aries is only Mars. Mars is in Libra and is in the star of Jupiter (Visakha star). Mars and Jupiter are aspecting 10th house, which will boost the 10th house. Saturn is in 06** house and is better placed being in the star of Venus (11). Let us go to the DBAS.

## DASHA

## The dasha is that Mbdrt-Mercury-Venus-Venus-Rahu-Rahu.

Moon is lagna lord and is in 11th house, which favours AAP. As far as BJP is concerned, Moon is in 05th house, which is not favourable for success, but it is just indicating politics. Mercury is in 05th house for AAP, which may not be good, but good for BJP since it is in 11th house. But Mercury is in the star Anuradha, which is ruled by Saturn. Saturn is favourable for AAP being in 06th house, but unfavourable for BJP being in 12th house.

## RP LINK

The dasha is that Moon-Mercury-Venus-Venus Rahu-Rahu. The RP are Moon, Saturn, Venus and Mercury. Dasha lord Moon is in RP. Bhukti lord Mercury is in RP. Antara and sookshma lord Venus is in RP. Prana and Deha lord Rahu is not in RP, but it signifies Mercury, who is in RP. Hence DBA planets and RP are linked.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is in Capricorn at 26 deg., and this area is ruled by Saturn and Mars. Moon is in Virgo at 18 deg. and this area is ruled by Mercury and Moon. Day lord is Moon and hora lord is Venus.

## ASTRO HINTS

Marriage and partner are seen generally from 07th house. Lagna is for self. 08th house is for magnalya, while 12th house is for shayana (sleep). 05th house is for love and planet Venus is for love. 02nd house is for family.

## RPVs QUERY

Day lord Moon signifies 07, which indicates marriage. Hora lord is Venus, which is the planet of love. It is also lord of 05th house from Capricorn (Makara lagna). 05th house is for love. These inputs indicate nature of the query.

## ANALYSIS

In this prashna Kundli, 05th lord is Venus. It is in lagna lord Saturn's star in Pisces. Venus is in mutual aspect with 07th lord Moon. Moon is in own star. Moon is female. Hence the boy is in love with a girl.

## DBAS

The question was asked during the VMD of Moon-Mercury-Moon-Ketu as per the Prashna Kundli.

---

Dasha and antara lord is Moon, who is 07th lord. Mercury signifies 02 and 04 indicating family and home. Ketu indicates 02nd house. Lagna lord Saturn is in 12th house and it is in Ketu star Moola. Rahu is in 08th house in Ketu star. Hence houses 01, 02, 04, 07, 08 and 10 are in the picture as per DBA in the Prashna chart.

It was therefore predicted to the father of the boy that the marriage between this boy and the girl whom he loves shall take place. After many deliberations, the parents agreed for marriage, which was solemnized in 2019.

## RP LINK

VMD is of Moon-Mercury-Moon-Ketu. RP are Saturn, Mars, Mercury and Moom. Dasha and Antara lord Moon is in RP. Bhukti lord Mercury is in RP. Sookshma lord Ketu is not in RP, but Is getting the aspect of Saturn. Hence the dasha planets are linked to RP.

## REMEDIES

There is no specific remedy prescribed for this case.

## RULING PLANETS (RP)

Lagna is at 09 deg. in Aquarius, and this area is ruled by Mercury and Rahu. Moon is at 11 deg. in Sagittarius, and this area is ruled by Jupiter and Ketu. Day lord is Mercury and hora lord is Venus. Culminating, the RP are Saturn, Rahu, Jupiter and Mercury.

## RPVs QUERY

The hora lord is Venus and it signifies 01, 04, 05, 06, 09, 08, 09, 10, 11 and 12 through self and Rahu. The query regarding health (05, 06 and 11) and longevity (01, 08 and Badhaka) are covered. Hence the hora lord tallies with the query.

RP Sun signifies 12 and it is in own star. Sun signifies Medicine, Doctor, etc. Jupiter, Ketu are in 11th house, while Mercury is in 05th house. Houses 05 and 11 are for recovery from disease. Hence the RP planets too tallies with the question.

## VIMSOTTARI MAHA DASAS

Ketu 2014-02-16 - 2021-02-16
Venus 2021-02-16 - 2041-02-16

## ASTRO HINTS

## Illness or health

Houses 01, 06 and 08 promises serious health issues. Saturn is roga karaka. 12th house is for hospitalization.

Houses 01, 05 and 11 promises recovery from disease. Fast moving planets can bring in quick recovery.

---

## ANALYSIS-ILLNESS

Planets signifying 06th house are Moon, Mercury, Rahu and Venus. Moon is in 11th house. Mercury is in 12th house.Rahu is in 05th house and Venus is in 11th house. Out of these planets, Mercury signifies 01, 06, 08 and 12 through self, Moon and Saturn. But Saturn is in 11th house. Since more of houses 05 and 11 are appearing, it is felt that there is no illness for the spouse, especially in Ketu maha dasha.

## ANALYSIS-LONGEVITY

08th house shall be in Virgo (Kanya), which is ruled by Mercury. Badhaka house shall be 09th house, which is Libra or Tula. Badhaka swamy is Venus, who is in lagna. It is in the constellation of Rahu (Satabhisha). Rahu signifies Mercury too. Hence Venus is linked to 08th house. Hence Venus is detrimental because of its significations like 01, 08 and badhaka.

But what about Mercury? Mercury is 08th lord. It is in the constellation of Moon, viz. Shravana in 12th house. Since houses 08 and 12 are linked along with Moon and Mercury, it is possible, person thinks of committing suicide.

However, Mercury is not literally linked to badhaka. Its star lord is Moon, who signifies 06, 11 and Ketu. Ketu offers significations of Saturn and Jupiter. So Moon also offers the result of Saturn and Jupiter through Ketu. This is because Ketu is conjunct with Saturn and Jupiter. They are in 11th house, which is considered good for longevity, since it is not badhaka.

---

Hence it is felt that dasha of Ketu which runs into 2021 is not dangerous.

The death is envisaged in the DBA of Venus-Saturn-Mercury in 2034, when the person shall be 68 years old. These planets signify houses 01, 08 and badhaka. 12th house is called house of emancipation. These planets do signify 12th house too.

## RP LINK

The RP are Sun, Ketu, Jupiter and Mercury. The dasha is that of Ketu-Venus. Dasha lord Ketu is in the RP. Bhukti lord Venus is not in RP, but Jupiter is in RP and it is in Venus star Purva Ashada.

## REMEDIES

For good health and longevity, gurus suggest wearing of Mahamrithyunjaya yantra.

They can pray in Lord Vishnu or Lord Venkateshwara temple on Wednesdays since Mercury is the 08th lord. Lal Kitab remedies suggest the following:

(a) New earthen pitcher with its top covered may be dropped in running water.

(b) Domesticate one dog, but its colour should not be brown.

# worship goddess Durga and seek blessing from small girl children below age of 09.
